HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000427/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1aa2cb990b2583cc7e1c","text":"4. **Lightness converts verdict into rapid trajectory.** `خ ف ف B002` supplies quick movement and departure. Immediately afterward, `ه و ي B002` and `ه و ي B008` in `هَاوِيَةٌ` supply falling into an abyss and fast thrown motion. These branches give `خَفَّتْ` the enabling role: what lacks counterweight is imagined as departing and falling without resistance. The grammatical sequence supplies result but does not state a physical law, so the lightness-to-fall arrow is inferred. The ayah reads dynamically as the start of a downward transition, not only as a scale reading.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_26f26d248ec786ac9d68","text":"101:9 made خفة kinetic and directional: the light scales do not merely fail; they send the person toward Hawiyah. 101:11 then made the lightness tragically moth-like, since the final destination is fire.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000427/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_292ff33816f012e2700d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:8:3\",\"root_000427\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Hafiflik, kişiyi bir sonraki ayetteki uçuruma karşı koyamadan hızla götüren hareket başlangıcına dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000427/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_6366c2ae6f9b028a3b43","text":"1. **Lightness is an assessed deficit, not release from burden.** `خ ف ف B001` supplies lightness against heaviness, while `و ز ن B002` supplies justice and accounting. The line mirrors `ثَقُلَتْ مَوَٰزِينُهُ` in 101:6 but leads through 101:9-11 to abyss and fire. The justice branch makes low weight a deficit within assessment rather than a welcome easing. The packet gives the contrast and outcome; I infer that the outcome fixes the negative direction of otherwise ambivalent lightness. The ayah therefore overturns the ordinary attraction of being light.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000427/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_67ec5542df683f654191","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:8:3\",\"root_000427\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Hakları önemsiz sayma boyutu, başkalarını hafife alanın kendisinin tartıda önemsiz bulunması biçiminde karşılık görür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000427/B003","root_001645/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_7f098cc2f6edeadc6694","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:8:3\",\"root_000427\",[\"B003\"]],[\"101:8:4\",\"root_001645\",[\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Azlık ve değer taşıyan konum boyutları birlikte, kişinin tartıda yalnız nicelikçe değil anlamlı bir ağırlık bakımından da eksik kaldığını gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000427/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8227e113af9297ef46a8","text":"The immediate `هَاوِيَةٌ` in 101:9 activated `خ ف ف B002` more strongly than the scale image alone: lightness becomes rapid departure into a fall. Yet `أُمُّهُ` also corrected a merely diffuse trajectory by giving it a center. The question and answer in 101:10-11 then close that trajectory inside a specified fiery environment, confirming that lightness here enables directed descent rather than free motion.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000427/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a016ad086d102716788f","text":"2. **Low quantity becomes lack of standing.** `خ ف ف B003` supplies fewness or reduced amount in a balance, and `و ز ن B007` supplies standing or value that carries weight, including the idiom of having no weight. Their co-presence assigns two levels to `خَفَّتْ`: insufficient measured content and insufficient significance. I infer that quantity and standing coincide in this judgment; the text does not state their equivalence. The line reads as a verdict that the person fails to register substantially, not merely that one pan moves upward.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000427/B001","root_001645/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a02da969404ebb710f1c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:8:3\",\"root_000427\",[\"B001\"]],[\"101:8:4\",\"root_001645\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Hafiflik, önceki savrulmanın tersine özgürlük değil, adalet tartısında kişiyi tutacak karşı ağırlığın yokluğudur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000053/B013","root_000202/B001","root_000427/B001","root_000427/B002","root_000427/B004","root_000427/B005","root_001609/B008","root_001645/B002","root_001645/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_bbc4058321a9b6069b3c","text":"1. Lightness as failed counterweight. Evidence: خ ف ف B001, lightness against heaviness; و ز ن B002, justice/accounting; ث ق ل B001 in 101:6, opposing heaviness. Structural cue: وأما creates the formal counterpart to 101:6. Mechanism: the same measuring apparatus that preserves worth now exposes lack of weight. Abductive move: I infer that lightness is privative: it is not freedom but insufficiency before justice. Reading change: خفت reads as devaluation rather than harmless lightness. 2. Agitated motion resumes. Evidence: خ ف ف B004, levity, foolishness, agitation; ن و س B001 and ف ر ش B005 in 101:4, swaying and fluttering; ه و ي B008 in 101:9, fast going/thrown travel. Structural cue: light scales are followed immediately by Hawiyah. Mechanism: moral lightness lets the person rejoin the earlier unstable motion, now as downward rush. Abductive move: I supply the kinetic consequence from underweight to falling. Reading change: the ayah becomes the pivot from weighing to plunge. 3. Contemptuous insignificance. Evidence: خ ف ف B005, contempt/disparagement and treating a right as insignificant; و ز ن B007, standing/value with weight; ء م م B013 later, slight/insignificant thing. Structural cue: the light case is answered by a terrifying \"mother\" rather than honorable life. Mechanism: what treated rights as light is itself found weightless and redirected to an abased origin. Abductive move: I infer a retributive mirroring between making things insignificant and becoming insignificant. Reading change: خفت موازينه can read ethically as the exposure of contempt. 4. Rapid departure from camp. Evidence: خ ف ف B002, swift movement/departure; ق ر ع B010, road/courtyard; ء م م B012 in 101:9, aiming/heading toward; ه و ي B002, falling into abyss. Structural cue: the verse's condition is immediately followed by destination imagery. Mechanism: the light one is not settled in a pleasing life but is hurried out toward the falling-place. Abductive move: I supply travel-direction from the following \"mother/Hawiyah\" answer. Reading change: the ayah reads as departure under judgment, not just a balance report.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000427/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_cc36099baeae5ef02a24","text":"3. **Earlier agitation becomes evaluative instability.** `خ ف ف B004` carries levity, rashness, and agitation. Within the focus window, people oscillate and flutter as scattered moth-like bodies in 101:4, while the paired scales later distinguish heavy from light. The branch gives the fixed lightness a behavioral-spatial echo: lack of settled orientation in the crowd becomes lack of settled weight in assessment. The packet does not identify the earlier crowd with this individual case, so that continuity is abductive.
